# Final report: real02

47 views: 15 polished, 32 Cycles (gate 2, gate_validation 5, room 20, vision_check 5). Stages: render run, gate validation ok, polish run, vision check run (calibration run). A polished image is final only when the gate accepted it and the vision check checked it without finding a lost or added element (§5.5). Mismatches are listed with their evidence and never auto-fixed.

## 3D files

Open in Blender: the `.blend` directly (textures packed, cameras with their metered exposure in the custom property `wenart_exposure_ev`, render settings as these images); the `.glb` with File > Import > glTF 2.0 (also other 3D tools). In the results: `final/<project>/3d/`.

| file | size |
|---|---|
| [real02.blend](3d/real02.blend) | 227.5 MB |
| [real02.glb](3d/real02.glb) | 651.7 MB |

47 cameras; textures scaled to at most 1024 px (172 scaled) for the download.

## AI decor

53 decor items chosen by the AI (Qwen/Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct; both passes agreeing) in 18 rooms; 22 items by the rules. The decor stage places decor only: it moves, adds and removes no furniture (the AI completion of furnished rooms has its own section, `AI completion of furnished rooms`; `furniture/<project>/decor_report.md` has every room).

- r_L-1_salon: rules (no slot for AI decor)
- r_L0_yatak_odasi_3: rules (no slot for AI decor)
- r_L0_yatak_odasi_4: rules (no slot for AI decor)

- camera cam_r_L0_oda_2: no free camera point in the room; no point clear of the walls and the tall proxies, camera at the room's inner point INSIDE a proxy
- camera cam_r_L0_oda_2_1: no free camera point in the room; no point clear of the walls and the tall proxies, camera at the room's inner point INSIDE a proxy
- 42 drawn piece(s) not typed: the two AI passes disagree or did not answer (unknown, unverified; footprint kept): f_L-1_022, f_L-1_024, f_L-1_033, f_L-1_034, f_L-1_035, f_L-1_036, f_L-1_045, f_L-1_046

- exterior gate polish_disabled: the exterior views are the Cycles render (negative controls rejected 0.846 < 0.90 (52 comparisons): the gate lets geometry changes through)

## AI completion of furnished rooms (Feature 1)

Mode `furnished_rooms: complete` (assumed: furnished_rooms, furnished_rooms_keep, furnished_rooms_keep_size, render.twin_rooms). Drawn pieces keep their anchor (+-5 cm) and front (+-1 deg); the AI may change a piece's type (within the room type's types), size, height and look (it stays `from_documents`, `modified_by_ai`, with the drawn type and size recorded) and add the pieces the room type misses (`added_by_ai`, never a second main piece). Fixed equipment never changes. A change needs both AI passes.

0 change(s) applied, 58 piece(s) added, 0 wall cabinet run(s).
